MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.35% (+52bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-21 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-20
Current:  2.35%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +52bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.31% (+48bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-20 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-17
Current:  2.31%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +48bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

What this means:
A wave of press coverage reports memory-chip stocks selling off on oversupply fears despite record AI-driven demand.

内容:
多家媒体报道，尽管 AI 需求创纪录，存储芯片股仍因供应过剩担忧而暴跌。

Why it matters:
A demand/price disconnect in DRAM/NAND/HBM is a direct AI-capex bubble-stress tell — if memory suppliers are guiding to oversupply even as hyperscalers claim insatiable AI demand, either the capex build-out is overbuilding memory capacity or the 'record demand' narrative is cracking first in the supply chain.

影响:
存储芯片（DRAM/NAND/HBM）需求与股价走势出现背离，是 AI capex 泡沫压力的直接信号——如果供应商已经在担忧供应过剩，而超大规模厂商还在宣称 AI 需求无止境，那要么是产能建设已经超前于实际需求，要么'创纪录需求'的说法正率先在供应链环节出现裂痕。

影响:
CoreWeave 考虑对冲内存成本，说明其预期 DRAM/NAND/HBM 价格将继续下跌——这与支撑 AI 基建资本开支叙事的“内存短缺”说法相悖，更像是供过于求的信号。作为 GPU 云服务的主要买家之一，如果 CoreWeave 都在为价格暴跌做准备，可能意味着撑起 AI 硬件利润率的内存供应紧张正在松动。
